10:T45087,<h2 id="v6">v6<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#v6"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<h3 id="react-router-v6-official-release">React Router v6 Official Release<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#react-router-v6-official-release"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>The v6.0.0 beta was promoted to a full release on November 4, 2021.</p>
<h3 id="upgrading-to-react-v168-or-higher">Upgrading to React v16.8 or Higher<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#upgrading-to-react-v168-or-higher"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>That said, since React ≥ 15 is compatible with react router v5 and above, you can upgrade React alone without also upgrading to v6.</p>
<ul>
<li>Sample code: <a href="https://github.com/seungahhong/react-router-tutorial">https://github.com/seungahhong/react-router-tutorial</a></li>
</ul>
<h3 id="advantages-of-react-router-v6">Advantages of React Router v6<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#advantages-of-react-router-v6"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>The bundle size has been reduced by about 70% compared to the previous version. (Does it also reduce build time??)</li>
</ul>
<p>The v5.1 size was 9.4kb, and it has been reduced to 2.9kb in v6.</p>
<p><img src="/blog-assets/2022/03/assets/12/Untitled.png" alt="Untitled"></p>
<p><img src="/blog-assets/2022/03/assets/12/Untitled1.png" alt="Untitled"></p>
<ul>
<li>Broad support for React Hooks (features such as HOCs have been removed)</li>
<li>Dropped support for legacy browsers (however, this can be handled by using polyfills)
<ul>
<li>IE 11 support
<ul>
<li>Add the following to package.json to support the IE11 browser</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">"browserslist"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">    "production"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">      "..."</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">      "ie 11"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    ],</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">    "development"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">      "..."</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">      "ie 11"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    ]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Add polyfills
<ul>
<li>react-app-polyfill (provided by Facebook)
<ul>
<li>A library that transpiles the various syntaxes used in React development</li>
<li>It only includes the necessary pieces such as Promise, window.fetch, Symbol, Object.assign, Array.from + [ IE9 Map, Set ], so it is small in size and lightweight</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// src/index.tsx</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Add at the very first line</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react-app-polyfill/ie11'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react-app-polyfill/stable'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// async,await,generator 문법사용</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> React </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Reduced code volume thanks to relative paths (match.url, match.path)</li>
<li>A unified standard for features
<ul>
<li>e.g.) Router → element consolidation</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="installing-react-router-v6">Installing React Router v6<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#installing-react-router-v6"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="solidity" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="solidity" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">npm install react</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">router</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">dom </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// react-router-dom@6</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3 id="changes-from-switch--routes">Changes from Switch → Routes<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#changes-from-switch--routes"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>Instead of selecting based on order like Switch, it now works by choosing the best-matching route</li>
<li>Switch → Routes change (a route must be a child of routes)</li>
<li>The exact prop has been removed
<ul>
<li>When a sub-path is needed, use path *</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>component, children, and render are unified into element</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// v6 이전</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> exact</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"/"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> component</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">Home</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Not Found&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>

<h3 id="changes-from-withrouter--hooks">Changes from withRouter → hooks<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#changes-from-withrouter--hooks"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>Hooks API changes due to the removal of withRouter
<ul>
<li>match: useMatch → current relative path (however, the property values differ, so verification is needed)</li>
<li>history: useNavigate is supported</li>
<li>location: useLocation (also supported since v5)</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>

<p><img src="/blog-assets/2022/03/assets/12/Untitled5.png" alt="Untitled"></p>
<h3 id="changes-from-usehistory--usenavigate">Changes from useHistory → useNavigate<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#changes-from-usehistory--usenavigate"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>useNavigate has replaced the useHistory hook</p>
<ul>
<li>useHistory return value (object) vs useNavigate return value (function)</li>
<li>Previously you called go, goBack, goForward, push, and replace methods depending on the use case, but these have been changed to an intuitive function call</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// v6 이전</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> React </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { useHistory } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react-router'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Navigation</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> history</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useHistory</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">        onClick</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">          history.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">push</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      ></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        Home</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">        onClick</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">          history.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">goBack</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      ></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        Go -1</span></span>

<h3 id="useroutematch-changes">useRouteMatch Changes<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#useroutematch-changes"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>Since useRouteMatch is gone, the match.path and match.url previously used to obtain the current URL are also gone
<ul>
<li>They are replaced with relative paths based on the current router</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// v6 이전</span></span>

<h3 id="navlink-changes">NavLink Changes<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#navlink-changes"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p>activeStyle and activeClassName are gone, replaced by the isActive prop</p>
<ul>
<li>activeClassName → className={({ isActive }) => (isActive ? styles.active : '')}</li>
<li>activeStyle={activeStyle} → style={({ isActive }) => (isActive ? activeStyle : {})}</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p>exact has been changed to end</p>

<p>The existing renderRoutes from react-router-config has been changed into a Hook called useRoutes.</p>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useRoutes</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // Route에서 사용하는 props의 요소들과 동일</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    { path: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, element: &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Home</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /> },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    { path: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'dashboard'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, element: &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Dashboard</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /> },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      path: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'invoices'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      element: &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Invoices</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      // 중첩 라우트의 경우도 Route에서와 같이 children이라는 property를 사용</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      children: [</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        { path: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">':id'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, element: &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Invoice</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /> },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        { path: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'sent'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, element: &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">SentInvoices</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /> },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      ],</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // NotFound 페이지는 다음과 같이 구현할 수 있음</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    { path: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'*'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, element: &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">NotFound</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /> },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  ]);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // element를 return함으로써 적절한 계층으로 구성된 element가 렌더링 될 수 있도록 함</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> element;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h2 id="migrating-to-routerproviderwith-createbrowserrouter">Migrating to RouterProvider(with createBrowserRouter)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#migrating-to-routerproviderwith-createbrowserrouter"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<p>Background for Adoption</p>
<p>Here are the reasons createBrowserRouter was introduced, along with its advantages:</p>
<ol>
<li><strong>Simplified routing configuration</strong>: With createBrowserRouter, you can declare the route configuration as a single object. This lets you manage route configuration in a simpler and more consistent way.</li>
<li><strong>Route data loading</strong>: createBrowserRouter provides features that make data loading easier to handle. You can load data for each route and process it through a loader function. This allows you to manage both data and route configuration in one place.</li>
<li><strong>Easy management of nested routes</strong>: Setting up nested routes is also more intuitive. createBrowserRouter provides a structure for managing nested routes declaratively and clearly.</li>
<li><strong>Error Boundaries</strong>: You can easily add error boundaries to each route. With createBrowserRouter, setting up error boundaries to handle errors that occur on a specific route becomes straightforward.</li>
<li><strong>Lightweight and optimized</strong>: React Router v6 has been optimized in many areas with performance and usability in mind. createBrowserRouter provides a structure that lets you take advantage of these latest optimizations.</li>
</ol>
<h3 id="client-side-routing">client side routing<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#client-side-routing"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>

<h3 id="nested-routes">Nested Routes<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#nested-routes"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>Since you can now specify relative paths when defining a path, nested routing can be implemented, and child paths are set as relative paths of the parent path.</p>
<ul>
<li>createRoutesFromElements(Configure nested routes with JSX)
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Configure nested routes with JSX</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">createBrowserRouter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  createRoutesFromElements</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"/"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Root</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"contact"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Contact</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">        path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"dashboard"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">        element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Dashboard</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">        loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">request</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">          fetch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/api/dashboard.json'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">            signal: request.signal,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">          })</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">        }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">AuthLayout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"login"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Login</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">redirectIfUser</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"logout"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> action</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">logoutUser</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  ),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>

<h3 id="dynamicsegments">dynamicsegments<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#dynamicsegments"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"projects/:projectId/tasks/:taskId"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// If the current location is /projects/abc/tasks/3</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // sent to loaders</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">params</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    params.projectId; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// abc</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    params.taskId; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 3</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // and actions</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  action</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">params</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    params.projectId; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// abc</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    params.taskId; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 3</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Task</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // returned from `useParams`</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> params</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useParams</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Random</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  match.params.projectId; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// abc</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  match.params.taskId; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 3</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3 id="ranked-routematching">ranked routematching<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#ranked-routematching"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>When navigating to the teams/new link, both routes match, but the ranking algorithm connects it to the /teams/new route.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"/teams/:teamId"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"/teams/new"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3 id="active-links">active links<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#active-links"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// isActive: user knows where they are (isActive)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// isPending: where they're going (isPending)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">NavLink</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  style</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">isActive</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">isPending</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      color: isActive </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'red'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'inherit'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">isActive</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">isPending</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> isActive </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'active'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> isPending </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'pending'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> ''</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">/>;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> SomeComp</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>

<h3 id="data-loading">data loading<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#data-loading"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>Data can be loaded during navigation through a loader.</p>
<p>In addition, the value returned by the loader can be retrieved in each element via useLoaderData.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// loader</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"/"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">request</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // loaders can be async functions</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> res</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> fetch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/api/user.json'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      signal: request.signal,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> user</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> res.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">json</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> user;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Root</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">":teamId"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // loaders understand Fetch Responses and will automatically</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // unwrap the res.json(), so you can simply return a fetch</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">params</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> fetch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`/api/teams/${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">params</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">teamId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Team</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  ></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">      path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">":gameId"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">      loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">params</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">        // of course you can use any data store</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">        return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> fakeSdk.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getTeam</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(params.gameId);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">      element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Game</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// useLoaderData</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Root</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
